Evaluation:
```
def erdos_objective(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=h.size
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```
Could use FFT for speed:
```
def correlate_full_fft(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); size=1<<(n+m-2).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b[::-1],size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:n+m-1]
    return c.real
```
But np.correlate for n up to maybe 2000 direct may be okay? Need many evals. Implement FFT. But note exact evaluator uses np.correlate; FFT may have small numerical differences, but objective max tolerance? Use np.correlate for final and small n. For optimization, FFT okay. Need correct orientation? We only need max; FFT of convolution with reversed b gives same as np.correlate. Let's verify: np.correlate(a,b,'full') = np.convolve(a,b[::-1],'full'). Yes. So use. Could have tiny imaginary. For objective during optimization, use FFT if n>128 else direct.

Projection function:
```
def project_box_sum(v, S):
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    lo=np.min(v)-1.0
    hi=np.max(v)+1.0? Need tau such that sum(clip(v-tau))=S.
    # As tau increases, sum decreases. clip range.
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(v-mid,0,1))
       if s>S: lo=mid else hi=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)/2
    return np.clip(v-tau,0,1)
```
Need ensure S between 0,n; yes n/2. Bisection 60 iter.

```
def convolve_real(a,b):
   L=len(a)+len(b)-1; size=1<<(L-1).bit_length(); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size); return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:L]
```

Implement `evaluate_binary_counts(h)`:
```
def correlate_counts(h):
    n=len(h); size=1; while size < 2*n-1: size*=2
    a=np.asarray(h,float)
    b=1.0-a
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b[::-1],size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:2*n-1].real
    return np.rint(c)  # for binary counts
```
Actually for binary, b=1-h. The correlation values are counts. Rounding to int. Max int. We can use float for sumsq.

For continuous h, use same FFT but no rounding.

Need ensure FFT correlation order matches np.correlate. We tested conv with b[::-1]. Using `np.fft.irfft(fft(a)*fft(b[::-1]))` gives linear convolution with zero padding if size >= n+m-1. For real. This equals np.convolve(a,b[::-1]), which equals np.correlate(a,b). Good. Need maybe small negative imag. Good.

But for n small, `np.correlate` direct exact and faster. Implement:
```
def corr_full(a,b,round=False):
   if a.size < 256: return np.correlate(a,b,mode='full')
   ...
```
For binary use `round` to int. But np.correlate of int returns int; FFT rounding maybe slight. Use `np.fft.rfft` with complex. Good.

Local binary search details:
```
def binary_local_search(n, seed, time_limit, target, rng):
   h = generate random balanced
   c = counts(h); mx = c.max(); sq = c@c
   best = ...
   patience = 2000
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time: break
      i = rng.choice(ones_indices); j = rng.choice(zeros_indices)
      # swap
      old_i=h[i]; old_j=h[j]
      h[i]=0; h[j]=1
      c_new=counts(h); mx_new=c_new.max(); sq_new=c_new@c_new
      if mx_new < mx or (mx_new==mx and sq_new < sq): accept
      else revert
      #Sometimes do double swap? Or random restart if no improvement patience.
```
Maintaining ones_indices dynamically after swap: just flip and if accept update lists; else revert. But if random choose from current lists, need efficient. For n≤128, can just `ones = np.nonzero(h)[0]` each iteration? Too slow. Maintain Python lists? Simpler: use `np.where(h==1)` occasionally. But 100k iterations, okay maybe. Or choose random i,j and check h[i]!=h[j]; if same skip. Probability 1/2? Balanced, choose random i,j different, probability different ~1/2. We can just do:
```
i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
if i==j or h[i]==h[j]: continue
h[i],h[j]=h[j],h[i]
...
```

def projected_gradient_step(h, grad, base_step=1.0):
   # choose step candidates based on grad norm
   gnorm = np.linalg.norm(grad)
   if gnorm==0: return h
   step = base_step / gnorm
   best_h=h; best_C=C_current

       step *= 0.7
   # If no accept, maybe try small random? return best

```
def direct_weighted_grad(h, weights_c): # weights_c aligned with np order
   n=len(h); s=n-1; grad=np.zeros(n); dx=2/n
   # For p=0..2n-2, shift = n-1-p
   for p,w in enumerate(weights_c):
      if w==0: continue
      sh = n-1-p
      # grad += w*dgrad for shift sh
      if sh>=0:
         # forward exists i=0..n-sh-1: grad[i] += w*(1 - h[i+sh])
         grad[:n-sh] += w*(1 - h[sh:])
         # backward exists i=sh..n-1: grad[i] -= w*h[i-sh]
         grad[sh:] -= w*h[:-sh]
      else:
         d=-sh
         # forward i=0..n-d-1? h_i(1-h_{i-d}); i-d valid if i>=d? Wait shift negative: i+s = i-d valid when i>=d. For h_i term, derivative (1 - h_{i-d}) for i=d..n-1.
         grad[d:] += w*(1 - h[:-d])
         # backward term: from -h_i h_{i-d}, derivative for variable j=i-d? variable j = i-d, i=j+d, condition i<n => j< n-d. grad[j] -= w*h[j+d]
         grad[:n-d] -= w*h[d:]
   return dx*grad
```
Let's compare with general formula for sh=-d: forward part sum over j where j+s valid: j-d in [0,n) => j>=d: (1-h_{j-d}); backward part: j-s = j+d valid => j<n-d: -h_{j+d}. Good.

Implement `weighted_gradient_from_weights(h, weights_c)`:
```
def weighted_gradient_from_weights(h, weights_c):
    n=h.size; grad=np.zeros(n); dx=2/n; off=n-1
    for p,w in enumerate(weights_c):
        if w <= 1e-15: continue
        s=off-p
        if s>=0:
            L=n-s
            grad[:L] += w*(1.0-h[s:s+L])
            grad[s:] -= w*h[:L]
        else:
            d=-s
            L=n-d
            grad[d:d+L] += w*(1.0-h[:L])
            grad[:L] -= w*h[d:d+L]
    return dx*grad
```
Check shift positive s: L=n-s. indices i=0..L-1: forward h[i+s]; backward derivative variable i+s: subtract h[i]. We do grad[:L]+=(1-h[s:]); grad[s:-?]= h[:L] negative. For s=0: both same; forward grad[:] +=1-h; backward grad[:] -=h => grad=1-2h? But derivative q0=∑ h_i(1-h_i) = ∑(h_i - h_i^2); derivative 1-2h_i. Yes.
For s negative -d: i=d..n-1 forward h[i-d]; i=0..L-1? Let L=n-d. We do grad[d:d+L] += 1-h[:L] (h[i-d]), correct for i=d..n-1. Backward: term -h_i h_{j-d}; variable j=i-d from0..L-1 subtract h[d:d+L]. Good.
Sum of grad for s=0 weights sum1: dx*sum(1-2h)=dx*(n-2sum)=0 since sum=n/2. Good.

def refine_subgradient(h, n, max_iter, time_limit):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
    C=exact(h)
    best=h.copy(); bestC=C
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time: break
       q = corr_values(h)
       w=active_weights(q, eps=1e-4)
       grad=weighted_gradient(h,w)
       if norm(grad)==0: break
       # line search
       step = 1.0/(np.linalg.norm(grad)+1e-12)
       # maybe scale by 0.5? h domain [0,1], step too large. Try candidates [step*0.01?]. Use factor.
       accepted=False
       for trial in range(20):

          Ctrial=exact(v)
          if Ctrial < C - 1e-10:
              h=v; C=Ctrial; accepted=True; break
          step *= 0.6
       if not accepted:
          stall+=1
          # perturb h by small noise
          if stall>20:
             noise = 0.05*rng normal? But no rng? pass seed.
       else: stall=0
       if C < bestC: best=v...

```
def iterate_comb_masks(n,m):
    mask=(1<<m)-1
    limit=1<<n
    while mask < limit:
       yield mask
       c=mask & -mask
       r=mask + c
       mask = (((r ^ mask) >> 2)//c) | r
```
This generates masks with m bits. Need ensure <1<<n. Fast in Python? Division by c maybe. For 705k okay.
Then evaluate:
```
def mask_max_count(mask,n):
   full=(1<<n)-1
   best=0
   for d in range(1,n):
      pos = mask & (((1<<(n-d))-1)) & ~(mask >> d)
      best = max(best, pos.bit_count())

  for restart in range(num_restarts until time):

     # maybe random mask: choose m positions using rng.choice(n, m, replace=False); build mask

     # local hill climb
     stalled=0
     while time not exceeded:
        # choose i,j random until different bits
        for attempts in range(50):
           i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
           bi=(mask>>i)&1; bj=(mask>>j)&1
           if i!=j and bi!=bj: break
           else continue
        swap = (1<<i)^(1<<j)
        new_mask = mask ^ swap
        new_mx,new_sq=mask_score(new_mask)
        accept = (new_mx<mx) or (new_mx==mx and new_sq<sq)
        # maybe accept equal if sq reduce; no.
        if accept:
           mask=new_mask; mx=new_mx; sq=new_sq; stalled=0; update best
        else:
           stalled += 1
           if stalled > 200:
              # random perturbation: do 5 random swaps unconditionally
              for _ in range(5): ...
              recompute; mx,sq=...
              stalled=0
              # possible to degrade best; keep best_mask separate
       # if mx lower than possible? break
```

def evaluate_solution(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=h.size
    if n<=0: return 1.0, h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # repair sum? maybe not for evaluation? The candidate should be feasible. For exact objective, use as is? If sum off, invalid. We'll ensure.
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```
For final, use this.

2. `repair_feasible(h,n)`:
If n differs, interpolate. Use positions `np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h_old.size)`; new similarly. If old size 1, fill. Then `np.clip`. Then target = n/2. Use `project_box_sum`.
```
def resample_to_n(h, n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    m=h.size
    if m==n: out=h.copy()
    elif m==1: out=np.full(n,h[0])
    else:
       x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
       x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       out=np.interp(x1,x0,np.clip(h,0,1))
    out=np.clip(out,0,1)
    return project_box_sum(out, 0.5*n)
```
For odd n target n/2 .5.

3. `project_box_sum(v,S)`:
Need robust. If S close 0 or n. Bisection. But if v already feasible, bisection still. Use vectorized.
```
def project_box_sum(v,S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    S=float(S)
    if n==0: return v
    if S <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    if S >= n: return np.ones(n)
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    # We want sum clip(v - tau)=S, decreasing in tau
    for _ in range(80):
       tau=(lo+hi)/2.0
       s=float(np.sum(np.clip(v-tau,0.0,1.0)))
       if s > S: lo=tau
       else: hi=tau
    tau=(lo+hi)/2
    out=np.clip(v-tau,0.0,1.0)
    # adjust tiny residue on a coordinate with room to make closer
    diff = S - float(np.sum(out))
    # distribute to elements not at bounds
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       idx=np.where((out>1e-12)&(out<1-1e-12))[0]
       if idx.size:
          add = np.clip(diff/idx.size, -min(out[idx],1-out[idx])? Could complicate.
       # just out += diff/idx.size and clip; projection may slightly off. Not crucial.
    return out
```
Bisection 80 gives sum error ~ very small. Residual adjustment maybe not needed. But if clamp boundaries? The bisection ensures. Use 60 enough. To ensure exact final, we can call a final `enforce_sum_exact(h,n)`: adjust one or two values.
But evaluator may check sum exactly? It says equivalently sum(h)==n/2 exactly? Probably numerical. We can make sum close <1e-12.

Adjust: After projection, compute `diff = S - sum(out)`. Find indices where 0<out<1. If diff positive, distribute as much as possible up to 1; if negative down. Use loop over sorted? Simpler:
```
if idx.size:
   per=diff/idx.size
   per=np.clip(per, -out[idx]+1e-15, 1-out[idx]-1e-15)
   out[idx]+=per
   # if residual, adjust one
```
But small; not needed.

4. `corr_values(h)`:
Use FFT for n>=128, direct else.
```
def corr_values(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=h.size
    if n < 128:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    size=1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()
    a=np.fft.rfft(h,size)
    b=np.fft.rfft((1.0-h)[::-1], size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(a*b,size)
    return c[:2*n-1].real
```
Potential FFT rounding for max values; okay. For final exact use direct maybe but n can be large. The evaluator uses np.correlate; if our FFT objective differs at 1e-12, final exact using same evaluate. For optimization slight error okay.

But for n large, size bit: `(2*n-2).bit_length()` gives bits needed; size=1<<bits maybe >=? If L=254, bit_length=8, size=256 good. If L exact power? e.g. L=254 bit_length8=256; good. If L=1 bit_length=1 size=2; okay. Could use `size=1; while size<2*n-1: size*=2`. Simple.

5. `exact_C(h)`:
```
def exact_C(h):
   h=np.asarray(h,float)
   n=h.size
   if n<512:
      corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
   else:
      corr=corr_values(h)
   return float(np.max(corr)*2.0/n)
```
Use direct for final? Evaluator uses np.correlate, but if n=1000 direct O1e6 okay for final few. Use np.correlate always for final maybe. But during repeated we use corr_values. `exact_C` can use `np.correlate` for n<=2000; for >2000 use FFT? But final h maybe n=45/90. Use direct.

6. `weights_active(q, eps)`:
```
def active_weights(q, eps=1e-6):
    q=np.asarray(q)
    w=np.zeros_like(q)
    mx=q.max()
    mask=q >= mx - eps
    cnt=mask.sum()
    if cnt==0:
       # softmax fallback
       return softmax_weights(q,10.0)
    w[mask]=1.0/cnt
    return w
```
`softmax_weights(q,beta)`:
```
m=q.max(); ex=np.exp(beta*(q-m)); s=ex.sum(); w=ex/s; # threshold?
```
Return c-order weights sum1.

7. `weighted_gradient(h, weights_c)`:
Direct loop as above. Need `n = int(h.size)`, `dx=2.0/n`, `off=n-1`. Use local `h_local = h`. For w extremely small skip. If weights_c from softmax has many small, threshold `1e-10`. But sum may be renormalized after threshold? We can do in weights function threshold and renormalize to save. Implement `filter_weights(w, thresh=1e-8)`:
```
s=w[>thresh].sum(); if s<1: w=w[>thresh]/s else w[...]
```
But if all small? not.

Gradient code:
```
def weighted_gradient(h, weights_c):
    n=h.size
    grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    if n==0: return grad
    dx=2.0/n
    off=n-1
    hw=np.asarray(h,float)
    for p in range(2*n-1):
        w=float(weights_c[p])
        if w <= 1e-12: continue
        s=off-p
        if s>=0:
            L=n-s
            if L>0:
                grad[:L] += w*(1.0-hw[s:s+L])
                grad[s:s+L] -= w*hw[:L] # Actually grad[s:] length L: grad[s:s+L] = grad[s:n]
        else:
            d=-s; L=n-d
            if L>0:
                grad[d:d+L] += w*(1.0-hw[:L])
                grad[:L] -= w*hw[d:d+L]
    return grad*dx
```
Check for s>=0 backward: grad[s:n] corresponds i=s..n-1 (L elements) subtract h[i-s] = h[0:L]. Good. For s=0, grad[:n]+=(1-h); grad[:n]-=h =>1-2h.
Sum zero? With weights sum1 and constraints sum=n/2, yes.

9. `continuous_refine_active(h,n,max_iter=200,time_limit=60)`:
Use exact C and active gradient. Need a random number for perturb. Use `np.random.default_rng(seed)`.
Algorithm details:
```
def continuous_refine_active(h,n,max_iter=200,time_limit=60.0,seed=0):
    h=project_box_sum(h, n/2).copy()
    C=exact_C(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); bestC=C
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall=0; eps=1e-5; step_scale=1.0
    deadline=time.time()+time_limit
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       q=corr_values(h)* (2/n)? Wait q should be integral values for weights? Objective C=q*dx. If q raw counts correlated, C=dx*corr. For gradient of C, weights on C same as weights on raw corr (dx factor common). We can use raw_corr or q_C. In weights, active if raw corr >= max - eps/dx? For exact C active uses C. Let's define cC=corr*2/n. Use cC for q. Gradient formula currently returns dx*grad = gradient of C? We used A_s (raw count) and multiplied by dx at end. That gives gradient of C. weights correspond to C values. So compute `q = corr_values(h) * (2.0/n)`.
       C=float(np.max(q))
       if C+1e-12<bestC: update
       w=active_weights(q, eps=1e-5)
       grad=weighted_gradient(h,w)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm<1e-12: break
       step = step_scale / gnorm
       # ensure not huge: h in [0,1]; max move 0.5 maybe. if step*gnorm > 0.5, step=0.5/gnorm
       if step > 100.0: step=100.0
       accepted=False
       for attempt in range(25):
           v=project_box_sum(h - step*grad, n/2)
           Cv=exact_C(v)
           if Cv < C - 1e-10:
               h=v; C=Cv; accepted=True; break
           step *= 0.65
       if accepted:
          stall=0
          if C < bestC - 1e-10:
              bestC=C; best_h=h.copy()
          # optionally update step_scale = min(2.0, step_scale*1.1)
       else:
          stall += 1
          # perturb
          if stall % 20 == 0:
             noise = rng.normal(0, 0.02, n)
             v=project_box_sum(h+noise, n/2)
             Cv=exact_C(v)
             if Cv < C + 0.05: # allow some worsening
                h=v; C=Cv
             stall=0
          if stall > 200:
             break
    return best_h,bestC
```
Subgradient may stuck; can increase eps to include near-active: if stall, eps *=2 up to 0.05? Let's add:
```
if stall>50: eps=min(0.1, eps*1.5)
```
Also after accepted, maybe eps=1e-5. Good.

But line search from active subgradient may not find any step if projecting to same. Could try random coordinate moves if no accept.

Maybe better to use TRF after a few iterations.

10. TRF functions:
Globals:
```
_TRF_N=0; _TRF_BETA=10.0; _TRF_Q_CACHE=None; _TRF_X_CACHE=None
```
But objective/jac separate recomputes. We can implement a cache by hash? For arrays, use id not stable. Use `np array_equal`? Too heavy. Just recompute. Set beta maybe 20.
```
def trf_objective(x):
    q=corr_values(x)*(2.0/x.size)
    return float(logsumexp_beta(q, _TRF_BETA))
def trf_jacobian(x):
    q=corr_values(x)*(2.0/x.size)
    w=softmax_weights(q, _TRF_BETA, thresh=1e-6)
    return weighted_gradient(x, w)
def eq_constraint_fun(x): return np.array([float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*x.size)])
def eq_constraint_jac(x): return np.ones((1,x.size), dtype=float)
```
`logsumexp_beta`:
```
def logsumexp_beta(q,beta):
   if beta<=0: return float(np.max(q))
   m=float(np.max(q))
   s=float(np.sum(np.exp(beta*(q-m))))
   return m + np.log(s)/beta
```
For beta large, m maybe 0.38, beta*(q-m) >=0? Actually q-m <=0? m max, so exp<=1, log <=0, value <=m, tends m. Good.

`trf_optimize(h,n,beta,maxiter,time_limit)`:
```
def trf_optimize(h,n,beta,maxiter=100,time_limit=120):
    global _TRF_N,_TRF_BETA
    _TRF_N=n; _TRF_BETA=float(beta)
    x0=project_box_sum(h, n/2)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_constraint_fun,'jac':eq_constraint_jac}]
    start=time.time()
    # If maxiter too many, TRF not time aware. Use maxiter 80.
    try:
       res=scipy.optimize.minimize(trf_objective, x0, method='trf', jac=trf_jacobian, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'gtol':1e-6, 'xrtol':1e-8, 'verbose':0})
       if res.x is not None:
           x=project_box_sum(res.x, n/2)
           return x, exact_C(x)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return x0, exact_C(x0)
```

12. Binary exhaustive functions:
Precompute masks per n? We can build in function and pass to search.

`build_pre(n)`:
```
def build_pre(n):
   full=(1<<n)-1
   low=[]; high=[]
   for d in range(1,n):
       low.append((1 << (n-d)) - 1)
       high.append(full ^ ((1 << d) - 1))
   return full, low, high
```
For n=128, high full^((1<<d)-1) huge int but okay. For d up to127.

`mask_count_max(mask,n,pre)`:
```
def mask_max_count(mask,n,pre):
    full, low, high = pre
    best=0
    # local variables for speed
    for idx,d in enumerate(range(1,n)):
       lm=low[idx]
       val=(mask & lm & ~(mask >> d)).bit_count()
       if val>best:
          best=val
          # early threshold? pass threshold maybe
       hr=high[idx]
       val=(mask & hr & ~(mask << d)).bit_count()
       if val>best: best=val
    return best
```
Could pass `limit` to break when best>=limit. If we only need improvement, yes. For exhaustive, `limit_threshold_mx`; if best reaches threshold, return best. But if best already >= threshold, no improvement. Use `if best >= target: return best`. For local, need exact max, no early.

Return mx,sq. No early.

13. Binary local search using masks for larger n. Need initial mask. Generate random balanced mask:
```
def random_mask(n,m,rng):
    # method: choose random permutation indices maybe rng.choice n,m replace=False. For n up to256, okay.
    idx = rng.choice(n, m, replace=False)
    mask=0
    for i in idx: mask |= 1<<int(i)
    return mask
```
For n=128 many restarts, choice overhead. Could use bitmask random bytes: For n<=64, `np.random`? Simpler. Alternative: start with all low bits, then perform random swaps to mix. `mask=(1<<m)-1`; then do `mix_iters` random swaps (set 1 at random low, zero at random high). This is faster and yields random structure. For n up to256, use Python int.
```
def randomize_mask(n,m,rng,mixes=200):
    mask=(1<<m)-1
    for _ in range(mixes):
       i=int(rng.integers(m)); j=int(rng.integers(m,n)) # high zero positions m..n-1
       mask ^= (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
    return mask
```
This starts block and random swaps; after 10*m maybe close random. Good.

Initial candidate functions:
- Random masks (several seeds).

- Harmonic threshold: Create array scores of length n, choose top m bits.
```
def harmonic_mask(n,m,rng,freq,phase):
   i=np.arange(n)
   score=np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*i/n + phase) + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*(freq+1)*i/n + phase*1.3)
   idx=np.argsort(score)[-m:]
   mask=0; for j in idx: mask|=1<<int(j)
```
Use for freq in 1..12? Might be slow if many but n small.

Let's outline run:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start=time.time(); deadline=t_start+budget_s
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # initialize best
    best_h=None; best_C=1.0; best_n=0
    # if initial_h_values exists
    try:
       parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
       if parent.size>0:
          parent=clip; parent=project_box_sum(parent, parent.size/2)
          C=exact_C(parent)
          best_h=parent; best_C=C; best_n=parent.size
    except NameError:
       # create h0 0.5 or random
       h0=np.full(32,0.5); best_C=0.5; best_h=h0; best_n=32
    # Stage exhaustive small binary
    if deadline - time.time() > 2:
       for n in [12,14,16,18,20,22]:
          if time_left small break
          allowed = int(np.floor((best_C - 1e-12)*n/2.0))
          lb = math.ceil((n//2)**2 / (2*n-2))
          if allowed < lb: continue
          mask_res = exhaustive_small(n, allowed, time_limit=min(30, ...))
          if mask_res: update best
    # Stage DE small
    for n in [8,10,12,14,16,20]:
       if time: maybe
       h=C=de_small_search(n, ...)
       if C<best: update
    # Stage binary local larger
    for n in [24,28,32,40,48,56,64,80,96,128]:
       if time: maybe
       if binary lower bound >= best_C: continue
       h,C=generate binary local
       update
    # Stage continuous parent
    if parent is not None:
       n0=parent.size
       # refine at n0
       if time: h_ref,C_ref=continuous_refine_active(parent,n0,time_limit=min(120,...)); update if better
       # trf

def weighted_from_proba(q,beta,thresh=1e-8):
  m=q.max(); ex=np.exp(beta*(q-m)); s=ex.sum(); w=ex/s
  mask=w>thresh
  if not mask.all(): w= w*mask; s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
  return w
```

Harmonic mask function:
```
def harmonic_mask(n,m,seed,f):
    rng=np.random.default_rng((seed,f))
    phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    phase2=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    score=np.sin(2*np.pi*f*i/n + phase) + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*(f+1)*i/n + phase2)
    idx=np.argpartition(score, m-1)[:m-1]? Need top m. Use `np.argsort(score)[-m:]`.
    mask=...
```

Let's refine functions.

`corr_values` exact with FFT:
```
def corr_values(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    b = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    size = 1
    need = 2*n - 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(h, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)
    return c[:need].astype(float, copy=False)
```
For n=257, FFT. Direct np.correlate O(n^2) for 256 ~65k, fine.

`exact_C`:
```
def exact_C(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); n=h.size
    if n==0: return 0.0
    corr=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)* (2.0/n))
```
For n large (maybe 512) direct max in final only; okay. If we call exact_C in continuous line search many, use `corr_values` maybe. Define `fast_C(h)` for iterative:
```
def fast_C(h):
   h=np.asarray(h,float); n=h.size
   if n==0: return 0.0
   corr=corr_values(h)
   return float(np.max(corr)*(2.0/n))
```
Use in optimization; final exact_C direct.

`fast_C` with FFT for large, direct for small. Good.

`project_box_sum` performance: For n small, bisection 60 with sum clip. Could optimize by if already feasible. Use 45 iterations enough. For n up to 512, 45*512=23k per call. DE 1000 calls=23M, okay. Use 50.
```
def project_box_sum(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).reshape(-1)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    S=float(S)
    out=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    cur=float(np.sum(out))
    if abs(cur-S) <= 1e-9:
        return out
    lo=float(np.min(v))-1.0
    hi=float(np.max(v))+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       tau=(lo+hi)*0.5
       val=np.clip(v-tau,0.0,1.0)
       s=float(np.sum(val))
       if s > S: lo=tau
       else: hi=tau
    tau=(lo+hi)*0.5
    out=np.clip(v-tau,0.0,1.0)
    diff=S-float(np.sum(out))
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       idx=np.where((out>1e-12)&(out<1.0-1e-12))[0]
       if idx.size:
          add=diff/idx.size
          # clamp add per coordinate
          add=np.clip(add, -out[idx]+1e-15, 1.0-out[idx]-1e-15)
          out[idx]+=add
       else:
          # adjust one coordinate with room
          idx2=np.where((out<1.0)|(out>0.0))[0]
          if idx2.size:
             i=int(idx2[0])
             out[i]=float(np.clip(out[i]+diff,0.0,1.0))
    return out
```
The add clamp with scalar `out[idx]` arrays: `np.clip(add, -out[idx]+1e-15, 1-out[idx]-1e-15)` if add scalar, returns array. But add=diff/idx.size scalar. Good. However if add too large, after clip residual remains. Could ignore. Sum close.

But in `idx=np.where((out>1e-12)&(out<1.0-1e-12))`, if out at bounds no room. fine.

`resample_to_n` uses `np.interp`; if old values sum maybe. Then project.
```
def resample_to_n(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).reshape(-1)
    m=h.size
    if m==0: out=np.full(n,0.5)
    elif m==n: out=h.copy()
    elif m==1: out=np.full(n,float(h[0]))
    else:
       x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
       x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       out=np.interp(x1,x0,h)
    out=np.clip(out,0,1)
    return project_box_sum(out, 0.5*n)
```

`ensure_feasible`:
```
def ensure_feasible(h):
   h=np.asarray(h,float).reshape(-1)
   S=0.5*h.size
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return np.full(h.size,0.5)
   out=np.clip(h,0,1)
   if abs(float(np.sum(out))-S) < 1e-8:
      return out
   return project_box_sum(out, S)
```

`logsumexp_beta`:
```
def logsumexp_beta(q,beta):
   q=np.asarray(q,float)
   if q.size==0: return 0.0
   m=float(np.max(q))
   if beta <= 1e-8: return m
   ex=np.exp(beta*(q-m))
   s=float(np.sum(ex))
   if s <=0: return m
   return m + float(np.log(s))/beta
```

`weighted_from_proba`:
```
def weighted_from_proba(q,beta,thresh=1e-10):
   q=np.asarray(q,float)
   m=float(np.max(q))
   ex=np.exp(beta*(q-m))
   s=float(np.sum(ex))
   if s <=0:
      # fallback active
      w=np.zeros_like(q); idx=int(np.argmax(q)); w[idx]=1.0; return w
   w=ex/s
   mask=w>thresh
   if not np.all(mask):
      w=w*mask
      s2=float(np.sum(w))
      if s2>0: w=w/s2
      else:
         w=np.zeros_like(q); idx=int(np.argmax(q)); w[idx]=1.0
   return w
```

`active_weights`:
```
def active_weights(q, eps=1e-6):
   q=np.asarray(q,float)
   mx=float(np.max(q))
   mask= q >= mx - eps
   cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
   if cnt <=0:
       return weighted_from_proba(q,10.0)
   w=np.zeros_like(q); w[mask]=1.0/cnt
   return w
```

`weighted_gradient` as above. To speed, if `weights_c` is sparse, we can get nonzero indices:
```
nz = np.flatnonzero(weights_c > 1e-12)
for p in nz: w=weights_c[p]...
```
Better than loop all lags. Use. If many, still okay.

```
def weighted_gradient(h, weights_c):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); n=h.size
    grad=np.zeros(n,float)
    if n==0 or weights_c.size==0: return grad
    nz=np.nonzero(weights_c > 1e-12)[0]
    off=n-1; dx=2.0/n
    for p in nz:
       w=float(weights_c[p]); s=off-int(p); ...
    return grad*dx
```
If weights_c from softmax threshold renormalized; good.

Potential memory for `nz` length 2n.

`continuous_refine_active`:
Need use `fast_C` for line search to avoid direct for large. But final update best use fast_C? At end exact. Use `fast_C` in loop. Ensure value comparable. Use tolerance.
```
def continuous_refine_active(h,n,max_iter, time_limit, seed=0):
   h=ensure_feasible(h).copy()
   C=fast_C(h)
   best_h=h.copy(); bestC=C
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   deadline=time.time()+time_limit
   stall=0; eps=1e-5; step_air=1.0
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      corr=corr_values(h); q=corr*(2.0/n)
      C=float(np.max(q))
      if C + 1e-12 < bestC:
         bestC=C; best_h=h.copy()
      w=active_weights(q, eps)
      grad=weighted_gradient(h,w)
      gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
      if gnorm < 1e-12:
         # noise
         h=project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0,0.05,n), n/2)
         C=fast_C(h); stall+=1
         if stall>50: break
         continue
      step = step_air / gnorm
      # limit step to move 0.25 norm maybe
      max_step = 0.25 / gnorm
      if step > max_step: step = max_step
      if step < 1e-4: step=1e-4
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(18):
         v=project_box_sum(h - step*grad, n/2)
         Cv=fast_C(v)
         if Cv < C - 1e-10:
            h=v; C=Cv; accepted=True; break
         step *= 0.6
      if accepted:
         stall=0
         if C < bestC - 1e-10: bestC=C; best_h=h.copy()
      else:
         stall += 1
         if stall % 10 == 0:
            noise_scale = 0.02 + 0.02*(stall//10)
            v=project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n), n/2)
            Cv=fast_C(v)
            if Cv < C + 0.05: h=v; C=Cv
         if stall % 40 == 0:
            eps = min(0.2, eps*1.5)
         if stall > 300: break
   # final exact
   bestC2=exact_C(best_h)
   if bestC2 < exact_C(h): return best_h,bestC2 else maybe h
```
Use `fast_C` in line search but `q` recompute; could set C from q max. Good.

Noise may violate best but keep best.

`slsqp_smooth_optimize`:
Need set global `_SMOOTH_BETA`. But if multiple runs, okay.
```
def slsqp_smooth_optimize(h,n,beta,maxiter=80):
   global _SMOOTH_BETA
   _SMOOTH_BETA=float(beta)
   x0=ensure_feasible(h).copy()
   if n==0: return x0, 0.0
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_constraint_fun,'jac':eq_constraint_jac}]
   try:
      res=scipy_opt.minimize(smooth_objective_scalar, x0, method='SLSQP', jac=smooth_jacobian_scalar, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-8})
   except Exception:
      return x0, fast_C(x0)
   x=ensure_feasible(res.x)
   # sometimes res.fun not; evaluate
   C=fast_C(x)
   # If res.x poor, keep best among x0 and res
   C0=fast_C(x0)
   if C < C0: return x,C
   if res.success is False and C0 < C: return x0,C0
   return x,C
```
But SLSQP may be slow; wrap time? Can't interrupt. Use maxiter based on n and available time. Could estimate. For n=90 maxiter 50.

`smooth_objective_scalar` uses `corr_values` and beta. `smooth_jacobian_scalar` uses same. To avoid recomputing, not critical.

`eq_constraint_fun` returns `np.array([float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*x.size)])`. `eq_constraint_jac` returns `np.ones((1, x.size))`. For x size maybe 0; handle.
```
def eq_constraint_jac(x):
    return np.ones((1, int(np.size(x))))
```

   C=fast_C(x); best=(x,C)

      update
      for beta in [5.0,10.0,20.0]: if time_left > 20:

      # exact refine active

      update

```

Implement `binary_local_search(n, time_limit, best_c, seed, parent=None)`:
```
def binary_local_search(n, time_limit, best_c, seed, parent=None):
    if n%2!=0 or n<2: return None
    m=n//2
    pre=build_pre(n)
    full,low,high=pre
    best_mask=None; best_mx=int(np.floor((best_c-1e-12)*n/2))+1 # one worse
    best_sq=10**18
    deadline=time.time()+time_limit
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=[]
    # randoms
    for r in range(4): starts.append(randomize_mask(n,m,rng))
    # thue
    starts.append(thue_mask(n,m))
    # harmonics
    for f in [2,3,5,8]: starts.append(harmonic_mask(n,m,seed+f,f))
    if parent is not None:
       starts.append(parent_threshold_mask(n,m,parent))
    # if time allow more random
    for mask in starts:
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       # ensure mask has m bits? functions do; randomize does.
       mx,sq=mask_score(mask,n,pre)
       Cval=2.0*mx/n
       if Cval < best_c and mx < best_mx:
           best_mask=mask; best_mx=mx; best_sq=sq; best_h=mask_to_array(mask,n)
       # local hill climb with time slice
       stall=0
       iter_count=0
       while time.time() < deadline:
          # If best_mx == lower bound and this mask max == lower bound? cannot max lower, but sq can still lower for same C? If same C not improve global but okay.
          if mx == best_mx and best_mask is not None:
             # we only care global; but local could reduce max below best_mx? if best_mx current.
             pass
          # choose swap
          done=False
          for _ in range(50):
             i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
             bi=(mask>>i)&1; bj=(mask>>j)&1
             if i!=j and bi!=bj: done=True; break
          if not done: continue
          new_mask=mask ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
          nmx,nsq=mask_score(new_mask,n,pre)
          if nmx < mx or (nmx == mx and nsq < sq):
             mask=new_mask; mx=nmx; sq=nsq; stall=0
             Cval=2.0*mx/n
             if Cval < (best_c if best_mask is None else 2.0*best_mx/n?) Actually update global best if Cval < best_c (outer) and mx < best_mx
             if Cval < best_c and mx < best_mx: best...
             # For local, track local_best maybe.
          else:
             stall += 1
             if stall == 150:
                # perturb 3 swaps
                for _ in range(3): random valid swap unconditional
                mask=...
                mx,sq=mask_score
                stall=0
             if stall > 1200: break
          iter_count +=1
    if best_mask is not None: return mask_to_array(best_mask,n), 2.0*best_mx/n
    return None
```
Need update best_c local? If a start finds improvement, subsequent starts can target lower. Use local_best_c. outer best_c not updated during function? We can update a local `current_limit_c = best_c` and when improve set. At end return. But outer updates after. Fine.

Potential issue: `mask_score` for every start/local evaluation recomputes all counts. For n=128 and 100k eval, okay. But if time limit 60s, may do millions? Need not too many. Python 25M loops maybe ~5-10s; 60s maybe 100M loops too slow. We should monitor and perhaps limit evaluations. Could add a per-evaluation overhead? Can't. Set time limit 30s per n. Maybe okay.

We can optimize `mask_score` by using local variables and while loop? Let's write efficient:
```
def mask_score(mask,n,pre):
    low=pre[1]; high=pre[2]
    mx=0; sq=0
    d=1
    for lm,hm in zip(low,high):
       pos=(mask & lm & ~(mask >> d)).bit_count()
       neg=(mask & hm & ~(mask << d)).bit_count()
       if pos>mx: mx=pos
       sq += pos*pos
       if neg>mx: mx=neg
       sq += neg*neg
       d += 1
    return mx,sq
```
Python for zip each iteration unpack tuple; okay. Could precompute list of tuples `pre_arrays`. Use `for d, lm, hm in pre_arrays:`. Build pre_arrays in `build_pre`:
```
def build_pre(n):
   low=[]; high=[]; arr=[]
   full=(1<<n)-1
   for d in range(1,n):
      lm=(1<<(n-d))-1
      hm=full ^ ((1<<d)-1)
      arr.append((d,lm,hm))
   return arr
```
Then `for d,lm,hm in arr:`.

`exhaustive_small` uses arr.

`mask_to_array`:
```
def mask_to_array(mask,n):
   h=np.zeros(n,float)
   # loop bits? For n up to128, okay. Could use bin.
   for i in range(n):
      if (mask>>i)&1: h[i]=1.0
   return h
```
For n=128, 128 loop. fine.

`randomize_mask` mixes low/high bits. Need ensure if m=0 or n? n even >0.
```
def randomize_mask(n,m,rng):
   if m==0: return 0
   if m==n: return (1<<n)-1
   mask=(1<<m)-1
   mixes = max(50, 3*n)
   for _ in range(mixes):
      i=int(rng.integers(m)); j=int(rng.integers(m,n))
      mask ^= (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
   return mask
```
After many swaps, mask still m bits. If i and j bits? i in low m bits initially ones but after swaps low bits may not all ones. Choosing i from 0..m-1 may pick zero; swapping with high may change count? If i bit 0 and j bit 1, swap still m bits. If both same, no change. It remains m bits because swapping one bit positions always preserves count regardless. Good. But if i/j same no issue. It mixes.

`thue_mask`:
```
def thue_mask(n,m):
   mask=0
   ones=0
   for i in range(n):
      if (i.bit_count() & 1):
         mask |= 1<<i; ones +=1
   if ones == m: return mask
   # adjust: if ones>m, zero out random high set bits; if ones<m, set random zero bits
   rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
   if ones>m:
      # need remove ones-m
      for idx in rng.choice(n, ones-m, replace=False):
         if (mask>>int(idx))&1: mask &= ~(1<<int(idx))
   else:
      need=m-ones
      # choose zeros
      zero_list=[]
      for i in range(n):
         if not (mask>>i)&1: zero_list.append(i)
      for idx in rng.choice(zero_list, need, replace=False) if len(zero_list)>=need else ...
   # ensure count m by random flips
   count=mask.bit_count()
   while count<m:
      i=int(rng.integers(n)); if not bit: set; count+=1
   while count>m:
      i=int(rng.integers(n)); if bit: clear; count-=1
   return mask
```
`int.bit_count()` for mask count. Could be slightly slow but fine.

`harmonic_mask`:
```
def harmonic_mask(n,m,seed,freq):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); phase2=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    idx_arr=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    score=np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*idx_arr/n + phase) + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*(freq+1)*idx_arr/n + phase2)
    # top m
    part=np.argpartition(score, m-1)[:m-1]? For m=n/2, m-1. Need include m-th. Simpler argsort.
    idx=np.argsort(score)[-m:]
    mask=0
    for i in idx: mask |= 1 << int(i)
    return mask
```
`np.argsort` length up to128, fine.

def binary_local_search(n, time_limit, best_c, seed, parent=None):
   if n<4 or n%2: return None
   m=n//2; exact_allowed=int(np.floor((best_c-1e-12)*n/2.0)); if exact_allowed < 0: return None
   lower_bound=math.ceil(float(m*m)/float(2*n-2))
   if lower_bound > exact_allowed: return None
   arr=build_pre(n)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   best_mask=None; best_mx=exact_allowed+1; best_sq=0
   deadline=time.time()+time_limit
   starts=[]
   ...
   per_start = min(20.0, max(3.0, time_limit/(max(1,len(starts)))) )
   for mask0 in starts:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       if best_mx <= lower_bound: break
       mask=mask0
       # ensure count? random/thue yes; harmonic/parent yes.
       mx,sq=mask_score(mask,n,arr)
       if mx < best_mx:
           best_mx=mx; best_mask=mask; best_sq=sq
       start_deadline = min(deadline, time.time()+per_start)
       stall=0
       while time.time() < start_deadline:
          if mx <= lower_bound: break
          # choose swap
          ...
          if accept:
             ...
             if mx < best_mx:
                best_mx=mx; best_mask=mask; best_sq=sq
                if best_mx <= lower_bound: break
          else:
             ...
   if best_mask is None: return None
   h=mask_to_array(best_mask,n)
   return h, 2.0*best_mx/n
```

Now, `de_small_search` may return a tuple or None. Implement:
```
def de_small_search(n, rem_time, seed):
   if n < 4 or rem_time < 3: return None
   if n <= 12: maxiter=100; popsize=15
   elif n <=16: maxiter=80; popsize=15
   elif n <=20: maxiter=60; popsize=12
   elif n <=24: maxiter=40; popsize=12
   else: return None
   if rem_time < 10: maxiter=max(5,maxiter//2)
   bounds=[(-3.0,3.0) for _ in range(n)]
   try:
      res=scipy_opt.differential_evolution(de_global_objective, bounds, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=popsize, seed=seed, tol=1e-4, disp=False, workers=1, polish=True)
   except Exception:
      try:
         res=scipy_opt.differential_evolution(de_global_objective, bounds, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=popsize, seed=seed, tol=1e-4, disp=False)
      except Exception:
         return None
   if res is None or res.x is None: return None
   h=ensure_feasible(res.x)
   C=fast_C(h)
   return h,C
```
If `rem_time` large but DE maxiter fixed; okay.

Potential issue: `differential_evolution` may take longer if `polish=True` uses Nelder-Mead, but okay.

def continuous_parent_stage(parent,bestC,time_limit,seed):
   h=ensure_feasible(parent); C=fast_C(h); best=(h,C)
   if time_limit < 30: return best
   # brief active to move
   h2,C2=continuous_refine_active(h,parent.size,max_iter=50,time_limit=min(30,time_limit*0.2),seed=seed+1)
   if C2<best[1]: best=(h2,C2)
   # SLSQP smooth
   for beta in (4.0, 8.0, 16.0):
       if time.time() >= deadline - 30: break
       maxiter = 80 if parent.size <= 60 else 40
       h3,C3=slsqp_smooth_optimize(best[0], parent.size, beta, maxiter)
       if C3<best[1]: best=(h3,C3)
   # final active
   h4,C4=continuous_refine_active(best[0], parent.size, max_iter=100, time_limit=remaining, seed=seed+2)
   if C4<best[1]: best=(h4,C4)
   return best
```

def final_repair(h):
    h=ensure_feasible(h)
    S=0.5*h.size
    diff=S-float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       idx=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
       if idx.size:
          # distribute diff over idx with clipping
          for _ in range(3):
             if abs(diff)<=1e-15: break
             per=diff/idx.size
             add=np.clip(per, -h[idx]+1e-16, 1-h[idx]-1e-16)
             h[idx]+=add
             diff=S-float(np.sum(h))
       else:
          i=int(np.argmin(np.maximum(np.minimum(h,1-h),0)))? skip
    return h

Implement:
```
def exhaustive_limit_mask(n, limit, time_limit):
    m=n//2
    if m<=0 or m>=n: return None
    arr=build_pre(n)
    lb=math.ceil(float(m*m)/float(2*n-2))
    best_mx=limit
    mask=(1<<m)-1
    lim=1<<n
    stop=time.time()+time_limit
    cnt=0
    while mask < lim:
       mx=mask_max_count_limit(mask,n,arr,best_mx)
       if mx < best_mx:
          best_mx=mx
          if best_mx <= lb: break
       cnt +=1
       if (cnt & 2047)==0 and time.time() > stop: break
       # next
       c=mask & -mask; r=mask+c; mask=(((r^mask)>>2)//c)|r
    if best_mx < limit: return best_mx
    return None
```
If time expires before finding optimum, returns best if found. Good.

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as scipy_opt

_SMOOTH_BETA = 10.0


def project_box_sum(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    S = float(S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    out = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    cur = float(np.sum(out))
    if abs(cur - S) <= 1e-10:
        return out

    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = np.clip(v - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(np.sum(val))
        if s > S:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau
    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    out = np.clip(v - tau, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = S - float(np.sum(out))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = np.where((out > 1e-12) & (out < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            add = diff / float(idx.size)
            add = np.clip(add, -out[idx] + 1e-15, 1.0 - out[idx] - 1e-15)
            out[idx] += add
    return out


def ensure_feasible(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return np.full(h.size, 0.5, dtype=float)
    out = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    S = 0.5 * float(h.size)
    if abs(float(np.sum(out)) - S) < 1e-8:
        return out
    return project_box_sum(out, S)


def resample_to_n(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    m = int(h.size)
    if m == 0:
        out = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    elif m == n:
        out = h.copy()
    elif m == 1:
        out = np.full(n, float(h[0]), dtype=float)
    else:
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(x1, x0, np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0))
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(out, 0.5 * float(n))


def corr_values(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    b = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(h, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)
    return c[:need].real


def fast_C(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    c = corr_values(h)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def exact_C(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def binary_lower_bound_c(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return 0.0
    m = n // 2
    denom = max(1, 2 * n - 2)
    return (2.0 / float(n)) * float(math.ceil(float(m * m) / float(denom)))


def logsumexp_beta(q, beta):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float)
    if q.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return m
    ex = np.exp(beta * (q - m))
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / float(beta)


def weighted_from_proba(q, beta, thresh=1e-10):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(q))
    ex = np.exp(beta * (q - m))
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(q)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return w
    w = ex / s
    mask = w > thresh
    if not np.all(mask):
        w = w * mask
        s2 = float(np.sum(w))
        if s2 > 0.0:
            w = w / s2
        else:
            w = np.zeros_like(q)
            w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
    return w


def active_weights(q, eps=1e-6):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    mx = float(np.max(q))
    mask = q >= (mx - eps)
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
    if cnt <= 0:
        return weighted_from_proba(q, 10.0)
    w = np.zeros_like(q)
    w[mask] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
    return w


def weighted_gradient(h, weights_c):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(h.size)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n == 0:
        return grad
    wc = np.asarray(weights_c, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if wc.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return grad
    nz = np.nonzero(wc > 1e-12)[0]
    if nz.size == 0:
        return grad
    off = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    for p in nz:
        w = float(wc[p])
        s = off - int(p)
        if s >= 0:
            L = n - s
            if L <= 0:
                continue
            grad[:L] += w * (1.0 - h[s:s + L])
            grad[s:s + L] -= w * h[:L]
        else:
            d = -s
            L = n - d
            if L <= 0:
                continue
            grad[d:d + L] += w * (1.0 - h[:L])
            grad[:L] -= w * h[d:d + L]
    return grad * dx


def eq_constraint_fun(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    return np.array([float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(x.size))], dtype=float)


def eq_constraint_jac(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    return np.ones(x.size, dtype=float)


def smooth_objective_scalar(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    q = corr_values(x) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return float(logsumexp_beta(q, _SMOOTH_BETA))


def smooth_jacobian_scalar(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    q = corr_values(x) * (2.0 / float(n))
    w = weighted_from_proba(q, _SMOOTH_BETA, thresh=1e-9)
    return weighted_gradient(x, w)


def exact_objective_scalar(x):
    return fast_C(x)


def slsqp_smooth_optimize(h, n, beta, maxiter=80):
    global _SMOOTH_BETA
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float), 0.0
    _SMOOTH_BETA = float(beta)
    x0 = ensure_feasible(h).copy()
    c0 = fast_C(x0)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': eq_constraint_fun, 'jac': eq_constraint_jac}]
    try:
        res = scipy_opt.minimize(
            smooth_objective_scalar,
            x0,
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=smooth_jacobian_scalar,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-8}
        )
    except Exception:
        return x0, c0
    x = ensure_feasible(res.x)
    cx = fast_C(x)
    if cx < c0:
        return x, cx
    return x0, c0


def continuous_refine_active(h, n, max_iter=100, time_limit=60.0, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float), 0.0
    h = ensure_feasible(h).copy()
    C = fast_C(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    deadline = time.time() + float(time_limit)
    stall = 0
    eps = 1e-5
    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        q = corr_values(h) * (2.0 / float(n))
        C = float(np.max(q))
        if C + 1e-12 < best_C:
            best_C = C
            best_h = h.copy()
        w = active_weights(q, eps)
        grad = weighted_gradient(h, w)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-11:
            v = project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n), 0.5 * float(n))
            Cv = fast_C(v)
            if Cv < best_C + 1e-9:
                h = v
            stall += 1
            if stall > 60:
                break
            continue

        step = min(0.5 / gnorm, 20.0)
        if step < 1e-6:
            step = 1e-6
        accepted = False
        for _ in range(18):
            v = project_box_sum(h - step * grad, 0.5 * float(n))
            Cv = fast_C(v)
            if Cv < C - 1e-10:
                h = v
                C = Cv
                accepted = True
                break
            step *= 0.6

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
            if C < best_C - 1e-10:
                best_C = C
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 10 == 0:
                ns = 0.02 + 0.01 * min(10, stall // 10)
                v = project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, ns, n), 0.5 * float(n))
                Cv = fast_C(v)
                if Cv < C + 0.05:
                    h = v
                    C = Cv
            if stall % 40 == 0:
                eps = min(0.2, eps * 1.5)
            if stall > 350:
                break

    c_exact = exact_C(h)
    if c_exact < best_C:
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_C = c_exact
    return best_h, float(best_C)


def de_global_objective(x):
    h = project_box_sum(x, 0.5 * float(x.size))
    return fast_C(h)


def de_small_search(n, rem_time, seed):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 4 or n > 24 or rem_time < 3.0:
        return None
    if n <= 12:
        maxiter = 100
        popsize = 15
    elif n <= 16:
        maxiter = 80
        popsize = 15
    elif n <= 20:
        maxiter = 60
        popsize = 12
    else:
        maxiter = 40
        popsize = 12

    if rem_time < 15.0:
        maxiter = max(8, maxiter // 3)
    if rem_time < 8.0:
        maxiter = max(5, maxiter // 2)

    bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0) for _ in range(n)]
    try:
        res = scipy_opt.differential_evolution(
            de_global_objective,
            bounds,
            maxiter=maxiter,
            popsize=popsize,
            seed=int(seed),
            tol=1e-4,
            disp=False,
            workers=1,
            polish=True
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = scipy_opt.differential_evolution(
                de_global_objective,
                bounds,
                maxiter=maxiter,
                popsize=popsize,
                seed=int(seed),
                tol=1e-4,
                disp=False
            )
        except Exception:
            return None
    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None
    h = ensure_feasible(res.x)
    C = fast_C(h)
    return h, C


def build_pre(n):
    n = int(n)
    arr = []
    full = (1 << n) - 1
    for d in range(1, n):
        low = (1 << (n - d)) - 1
        high = full ^ ((1 << d) - 1)
        arr.append((d, low, high))
    return arr


def mask_to_array(mask, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        if (mask >> i) & 1:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h


def mask_max_count_limit(mask, n, pre, limit):
    best = 0
    for d, low, high in pre:
        pos = (mask & low & ~(mask >> d)).bit_count()
        if pos > best:
            best = pos
            if best >= limit:
                return best
        neg = (mask & high & ~(mask << d)).bit_count()
        if neg > best:
            best = neg
            if best >= limit:
                return best
    return best


def mask_score(mask, n, pre):
    mx = 0
    sq = 0
    for d, low, high in pre:
        pos = (mask & low & ~(mask >> d)).bit_count()
        neg = (mask & high & ~(mask << d)).bit_count()
        if pos > mx:
            mx = pos
        if neg > mx:
            mx = neg
        sq += pos * pos + neg * neg
    return mx, sq


def exhaustive_limit_mask(n, limit, time_limit):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 4 or n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0 or m >= n or limit <= 0:
        return None
    pre = build_pre(n)
    lb = math.ceil(float(m * m) / float(max(1, 2 * n - 2)))
    best_mx = int(limit)
    best_mask = None
    mask = (1 << m) - 1
    lim = 1 << n
    stop = time.time() + float(time_limit)
    cnt = 0
    while mask < lim:
        mx = mask_max_count_limit(mask, n, pre, best_mx)
        if mx < best_mx:
            best_mx = mx
            best_mask = mask
            if best_mx <= lb:
                break
        cnt += 1
        if (cnt & 2047) == 0 and time.time() > stop:
            break
        c = mask & -mask
        r = mask + c
        mask = (((r ^ mask) >> 2) // c) | r
    if best_mask is not None and best_mx < int(limit):
        return best_mask, best_mx
    return None


def random_valid_swap(mask, n, rng):
    for _ in range(60):
        i = int(rng.integers(n))
        j = int(rng.integers(n))
        if i != j and ((mask >> i) & 1) != ((mask >> j) & 1):
            return i, j
    return None


def randomize_mask(n, m, rng):
    n = int(n)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return 0
    if m >= n:
        return (1 << n) - 1
    mask = (1 << m) - 1
    mixes = max(60, 3 * n)
    for _ in range(mixes):
        i = int(rng.integers(m))
        j = int(rng.integers(m, n))
        mask ^= (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
    return mask


def thue_mask(n, m):
    n = int(n)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return 0
    if m >= n:
        return (1 << n) - 1
    mask = 0
    ones = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if (i.bit_count() & 1):
            mask |= (1 << i)
            ones += 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    if ones < m:
        cand = []
        for i in range(n):
            if ((mask >> i) & 1) == 0:
                cand.append(i)
        if cand:
            for idx in rng.choice(np.asarray(cand), size=min(m - ones, len(cand)), replace=False):
                mask |= (1 << int(idx))
    elif ones > m:
        cand = []
        for i in range(n):
            if (mask >> i) & 1:
                cand.append(i)
        if cand:
            for idx in rng.choice(np.asarray(cand), size=min(ones - m, len(cand)), replace=False):
                mask &= ~(1 << int(idx))
    cnt = mask.bit_count()
    while cnt < m:
        i = int(rng.integers(n))
        if ((mask >> i) & 1) == 0:
            mask |= (1 << i)
            cnt += 1
    while cnt > m:
        i = int(rng.integers(n))
        if (mask >> i) & 1:
            mask &= ~(1 << i)
            cnt -= 1
    return mask


def harmonic_mask(n, m, seed, freq):
    n = int(n)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return 0
    if m >= n:
        return (1 << n) - 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    phase1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    phase2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    score = (
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x / float(n) + phase1)
        + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq + 1) * x / float(n) + phase2)
    )
    idx = np.argsort(score)[-m:]
    mask = 0
    for i in idx:
        mask |= (1 << int(i))
    return mask


def parent_threshold_mask(n, m, parent):
    n = int(n)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return 0
    if m >= n:
        return (1 << n) - 1
    if parent is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(777)
        return randomize_mask(n, m, rng)
    h = resample_to_n(parent, n)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[-m:]
    mask = 0
    for i in idx:
        mask |= (1 << int(i))
    return mask


def binary_local_search(n, time_limit, best_c, seed, parent=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 4 or n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    m = n // 2
    allowed = int(np.floor((float(best_c) - 1e-12) * n / 2.0))
    if allowed <= 0:
        return None
    lb = math.ceil(float(m * m) / float(max(1, 2 * n - 2)))
    if lb > allowed:
        return None
    pre = build_pre(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    deadline = time.time() + float(time_limit)

    starts = []
    for r in range(4):
        starts.append(randomize_mask(n, m, rng))
    starts.append(thue_mask(n, m))
    for f in (2, 3, 5, 8):
        starts.append(harmonic_mask(n, m, int(seed) + 100 * f, f))
    starts.append(parent_threshold_mask(n, m, parent))

    best_mask = None
    best_mx = allowed + 1
    best_sq = 0
    per_start = min(18.0, max(2.5, float(time_limit) / max(1, len(starts) + 1)))

    for mask0 in starts:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        if best_mx <= lb:
            break
        mask = int(mask0)
        cnt = mask.bit_count()
        if cnt != m:
            mask = randomize_mask(n, m, rng)
            mask = int(mask)
        mx, sq = mask_score(mask, n, pre)
        if mx < best_mx:
            best_mx = mx
            best_mask = mask
            best_sq = sq

        start_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + per_start)
        stall = 0
        while time.time() < start_deadline:
            if mx <= lb:
                break
            swapres = random_valid_swap(mask, n, rng)
            if swapres is None:
                break
            i, j = swapres
            new_mask = mask ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
            nmx, nsq = mask_score(new_mask, n, pre)
            if nmx < mx or (nmx == mx and nsq < sq):
                mask = new_mask
                mx = nmx
                sq = nsq
                stall = 0
                if mx < best_mx:
                    best_mx = mx
                    best_mask = mask
                    best_sq = sq
                    if best_mx <= lb:
                        break
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall == 180:
                    for _ in range(3):
                        sres = random_valid_swap(mask, n, rng)
                        if sres is None:
                            break
                        ii, jj = sres
                        mask ^= (1 << ii) ^ (1 << jj)
                    mx, sq = mask_score(mask, n, pre)
                    stall = 0
                if stall > 1500:
                    break

    if best_mask is not None:
        h = mask_to_array(best_mask, n)
        return h, float(2.0 * best_mx / float(n))
    return None


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 1300.0
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s <= 1.0:
        budget_s = 1300.0
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    parent = None
    best_h = None
    best_C = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        parent_arr = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        if parent_arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(parent_arr)):
            parent = ensure_feasible(parent_arr)
            pc = fast_C(parent)
            best_h = parent.copy()
            best_C = float(pc)
            best_n = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if best_h is None:
        n0 = 16
        m0 = n0 // 2
        mask0 = thue_mask(n0, m0)
        best_h = mask_to_array(mask0, n0)
        best_C = fast_C(best_h)
        best_n = int(best_h.size)

    ex_time = min(90.0, max(5.0, 0.07 * budget_s))
    if deadline - time.time() > ex_time + 5.0:
        ex_deadline = time.time() + ex_time
        for n in (12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 22):
            if time.time() >= ex_deadline - 2.0:
                break
            if n % 2 != 0:
                continue
            if binary_lower_bound_c(n) >= best_C - 1e-12:
                continue
            allowed = int(np.floor((best_C - 1e-12) * n / 2.0))
            lb_mx = math.ceil(float((n // 2) * (n // 2)) / float(max(1, 2 * n - 2)))
            if allowed < lb_mx:
                continue
            rem = min(30.0, ex_deadline - time.time())
            if rem <= 1.0:
                break
            res = exhaustive_limit_mask(n, allowed + 1, rem)
            if res is not None:
                mask, mx = res
                h = mask_to_array(mask, n)
                c = fast_C(h)
                if c < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h = h
                    best_C = float(c)
                    best_n = int(h.size)

    de_time = min(90.0, max(5.0, 0.07 * budget_s))
    if deadline - time.time() > de_time + 5.0:
        de_deadline = time.time() + de_time
        for n in (8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 24):
            if time.time() >= de_deadline - 5.0:
                break
            rem = de_deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 5.0:
                break
            res = de_small_search(n, rem, int(seed) + n)
            if res is not None:
                h, c = res
                if c < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h = h
                    best_C = float(c)
                    best_n = int(h.size)

    bin_time = min(500.0, max(20.0, 0.40 * budget_s))
    if deadline - time.time() > bin_time + 60.0:
        bin_deadline = time.time() + bin_time
        for n in (24, 28, 32, 40, 48, 56, 64, 80, 96, 128):
            if time.time() >= bin_deadline - 30.0:
                break
            if n % 2 != 0:
                continue
            if binary_lower_bound_c(n) >= best_C - 1e-12:
                continue
            rem = bin_deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 10.0:
                break
            limit = min(45.0, max(10.0, 0.5 * rem))
            res = binary_local_search(n, limit, best_C, int(seed) + 10000 + n, parent=parent)
            if res is not None:
                h, c = res
                if c < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h = h
                    best_C = float(c)
                    best_n = int(h.size)

    cont_deadline = deadline - 30.0
    if parent is not None and cont_deadline - time.time() > 60.0:
        cur_h = ensure_feasible(parent)
        cur_c = fast_C(cur_h)
        if cur_c < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h = cur_h.copy()
            best_C = float(cur_c)
            best_n = int(cur_h.size)

        rem = cont_deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 30.0:
            cand_h, cand_c = continuous_refine_active(
                cur_h, parent.size, max_iter=60, time_limit=min(60.0, 0.3 * rem), seed=int(seed) + 205
            )
            if cand_c < cur_c:
                cur_h, cur_c = cand_h, cand_c
                if cur_c < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h = cur_h.copy()
                    best_C = float(cur_c)
                    best_n = int(cur_h.size)

        for beta in (4.0, 8.0, 16.0):
            rem = cont_deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 30.0:
                break
            if parent.size <= 180:
                maxiter = 80 if parent.size <= 60 else 40
                cand_h, cand_c = slsqp_smooth_optimize(cur_h, parent.size, beta, maxiter)
                if cand_c < cur_c:
                    cur_h, cur_c = cand_h, cand_c
                    if cur_c < best_C - 1e-12:
                        best_h = cur_h.copy()
                        best_C = float(cur_c)
                        best_n = int(cur_h.size)

        rem = cont_deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 45.0:
            cand_h, cand_c = continuous_refine_active(
                cur_h, parent.size, max_iter=120, time_limit=min(180.0, 0.8 * rem), seed=int(seed) + 305
            )
            if cand_c < cur_c:
                cur_h, cur_c = cand_h, cand_c
                if cur_c < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h = cur_h.copy()
                    best_C = float(cur_c)
                    best_n = int(cur_h.size)

        for n_up in (90, 180):
            rem = cont_deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 80.0:
                break
            if n_up <= parent.size or n_up > 256:
                continue
            up_h = resample_to_n(cur_h, n_up)
            if n_up <= 128 and rem > 90.0:
                cand_h, cand_c = slsqp_smooth_optimize(up_h, n_up, 8.0, 40)
                if cand_c < cur_c:
                    cur_h, cur_c = cand_h, cand_c
                    if cur_c < best_C - 1e-12:
                        best_h = cur_h.copy()
                        best_C = float(cur_c)
                        best_n = int(cur_h.size)
            rem = cont_deadline - time.time()
            if rem > 60.0:
                cand_h, cand_c = continuous_refine_active(
                    cur_h if cur_h.size == n_up else up_h,
                    n_up,
                    max_iter=80,
                    time_limit=min(120.0, 0.7 * rem),
                    seed=int(seed) + 405 + n_up
                )
                if cand_c < cur_c:
                    cur_h, cur_c = cand_h, cand_c
                    if cur_c < best_C - 1e-12:
                        best_h = cur_h.copy()
                        best_C = float(cur_c)
                        best_n = int(cur_h.size)

    best_h = project_box_sum(np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).reshape(-1), 0.5 * float(best_h.size))
    best_C = exact_C(best_h)
    if not np.isfinite(best_C):
        best_h = np.full(max(45, best_n), 0.5, dtype=float)
        best_C = exact_C(best_h)
    best_n = int(best_h.size)
    return (best_h, float(best_C), best_n)
```
